Auto-defaults (preferences) 28

  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share' or Revenue Type is 'Variable'
    → Rev Share1, Monthly Minimum1, and Minimum Commit True Up Frequency are auto-set to 0/0/'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Impressions'
    → Impressions is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'LiveRamp Units'
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Records Under Management'
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is 'Impressions' and quantity < 65
    → Impressions is auto-set to '30M Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 65 and < 150
    → Impressions is auto-set to '65M Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 150 and < 260
    → Impressions is auto-set to '150M Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 260 and < 400
    → Impressions is auto-set to '260M Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 400 and < 575
    → Impressions is auto-set to '400M Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 575 and < 850
    → Impressions is auto-set to '575M Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 850 and < 1000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '850M Impressions'
  • When: quantity == 1000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '1B Impressions'
  • When: quantity > 1000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '>1B Impressions'
  • When: Unit of Measure is 'LiveRamp Units' and quantity < 10
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '5M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 10 and < 25
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '10M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 25 and < 50
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '25M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 50 and < 75
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '50M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 75 and < 100
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '75M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 100 and < 250
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '100M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 250 and < 500
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '250M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 500 and < 750
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '500M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 750 and < 1000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '750M LRUs'
  • When: quantity == 1000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '1B LRUs'
  • When: quantity > 1000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '> 1B LRUs'
  • When: Unit of Measure is 'Records Under Management', quantity tiers from <10 through ==300/>300
    → RUM Tier auto-set to matching band from '5M RUM' up to '>300M RUM (Custom Pricing Needed)'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share'
    → Rev Share1 is auto-set to 0
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share'
    → Monthly Minimum1 is auto-set to 0
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Records Under Management'
    → Package is auto-set to 'None'

  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share' or Revenue Type is 'Variable'
    → Rev Share1, Monthly Minimum1, and Minimum Commit True Up Frequency are auto-set to 0/0/'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Input Records'
    → Input Records V2 is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: qty < 25
    → Input Records V2 is auto-set to '10M Input Records'
  • When: qty >= 25 and qty < 50
    → 25M
  • When: qty >= 50 and qty < 75
    → 50M
  • When: qty >= 75 and qty < 100
    → 75M
  • When: qty >= 100 and qty < 250
    → 100M
  • When: qty >= 250 and qty < 500
    → 250M
  • When: qty >= 500 and qty < 750
    → 500M
  • When: qty >= 750 and qty < 1000
    → 750M
  • When: qty == 1000
    → 1B
  • When: qty > 1000
    → >1B

    “When Revenue Type is Variable or Unit of Measure is Revenue Share , quantity must be set to 1.”
  • error

  • error

    “Minimum Commit should be greater than $0 if Revenue Type is Fixed”
  • error

  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty < 105
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 45M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty >= 105 and qty < 190
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 105M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty >= 190 and qty < 300
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 190M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty >= 300 and qty < 400
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 300M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty >= 400 and qty < 575
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 400M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty >= 575 and qty < 850
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 575M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty is 850
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: 850M Impressions"
  • When: UnitOfMeasure is "Impressions" and RetailType is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc" and qty > 850
    → Retail_Type_and_Tier2 is "Retail - Non-Luxury Items / Electronics / Travels / Etc: >850M Impressions"

Validation messages 15

  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is 'Revenue Share' and Minimum Commit True Up Frequency is 'None' and Revenue Type is not 'Variable'
    “Minimum Commit True Up Frequency must be selected; 'None' is not a valid option. Please select a valid option before saving.”
  • error
    When: (Revenue Type is 'Variable' or Unit of Measure is 'Revenue Share') and quantity is not 1
    “When Revenue Type is Variable or Unit of Measure is Revenue Share , quantity must be set to 1.”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is 'Revenue Share' and Rev Share1 <= 0
    “Rev Share % is Required and can't be 0”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is 'Revenue Share' and Monthly Minimum1 <= 0 and Revenue Type is 'Fixed'
    “Minimum Commit should be greater than $0 if Revenue Type is Fixed”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share' and Rev Share1 is not 0
    “Rev Share % must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share' and Monthly Minimum1 is not 0
    “Minimum Commit must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.”
  • warning
    When: Unit of Measure is 'Records Under Management' and RUM Tier is 'None'
    “Please select a valid RUM Tier value.”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is not 'Records Under Management' and RUM Tier is not 'None'
    “If 'Records Under Management' is not selected on 'Unit of Measure', 'RUM Tier' must be set to 'None'”
  • warning
    When: Unit of Measure is 'Impressions' and Impressions is 'None'
    “Please select a valid Impressions value.”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is not 'Impressions' and Impressions is not 'None'
    “If 'Impressions' is not selected on 'Unit of Measure', 'Impressions' must be set to 'None'”
  • warning
    When: Unit of Measure is 'LiveRamp Units' and LiveRamp Units V2 is 'None'
    “Please select a valid LiveRamp Units value.”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is not 'LiveRamp Units' and LiveRamp Units V2 is not 'None'
    “If 'LiveRamp Units' is not selected on 'Unit of Measure', 'LiveRamp Units' must be set to 'None'”
  • error
    When: Unit of Measure is 'None'
    “Please select a valid Unit of Measure.”
  • error
    When: Pricing Type is not 'Token' and Revenue Type is 'None'
    “Please select a valid Revenue Type”
  • Info
    When: Impressions is '>2M Impressions' or LiveRamp Units V2 is '>10M LRUs' or RUM Tier is '>1M RUM'
    “A support case will be automatically created for below conditions: RUM Tier (>1M), LiveRamp Units (>10M), or Impressions (>2M)”

Auto-defaults (preferences) 19

  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Impressions'
    → Impressions is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'LiveRamp Units'
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Records Under Management'
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: Unit of Measure is 'Impressions' and quantity < 1000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '500k Impressions'
  • When: quantity >= 1000 and < 2000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '1M Impressions'
  • When: quantity == 2000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '2M Impressions'
  • When: quantity > 2000
    → Impressions is auto-set to '>2M Impressions'
  • When: Unit of Measure is 'LiveRamp Units' and quantity < 1000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '500k LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 1000 and < 5000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '1M LRUs'
  • When: quantity >= 5000 and < 10000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '5M LRUs'
  • When: quantity == 10000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '10M LRUs'
  • When: quantity > 10000
    → LiveRamp Units V2 is auto-set to '>10M LRUs'
  • When: Unit of Measure is 'Records Under Management' and quantity < 200
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to '100k RUM'
  • When: quantity >= 200 and < 500
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to '200k RUM'
  • When: quantity >= 500 and < 750
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to '500k RUM'
  • When: quantity >= 750 and < 1000
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to '750k RUM'
  • When: quantity == 1000
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to '1M RUM'
  • When: quantity > 1000
    → RUM Tier is auto-set to '>1M RUM'
  • When: Unit of Measure is not 'Revenue Share' or Revenue Type is 'Variable'
    → Rev Share1, Monthly Minimum1, and Minimum Commit True Up Frequency are auto-set to 0/0/'None'

    → Rev Share1, Monthly Minimum1, and Minimum Commit True Up Frequency are auto-set to 0/0/'None'

    → Identity API Records Processed V2 is auto-set to 'None'
  • When: qty < 50
    → Identity API Records Processed V2 is auto-set to '25M Identity API Records Processed'
  • When: qty >= 50 and qty < 75
    → 50M
  • When: qty >= 75 and qty < 100
    → 75M
  • When: qty >= 100 and qty < 250
    → 100M
  • When: qty >= 250 and qty < 500
    → 250M
  • When: qty >= 500 and qty < 750
    → 500M
  • When: qty >= 750 and qty < 1000
    → 750M
  • When: qty == 1000
    → 1B
  • When: qty > 1000
    → One Partner Unlimited

    → Input Records V2 is auto-set to '75M Input Records'

    → Input Records V2 is auto-set to '500M Input Records'

    → Input Records V2 is auto-set to '750M Input Records'

  • When: Unit of Measure is 'Input Records' and qty > 1000
